closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    //   // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n//        const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for      } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n candidate clusters for the round have been grown, the\n//     host picks the seed producing the largest cluster (matching the\n//     original algorithm's tie-break of first-largest-in-index-order),\n//     commits it, marks its members as clustered on the device, and the\n//     next round begins.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cfloat>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                       \\\n    do {                                                                       \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,   \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__));                                \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                      \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// GPU kernel: compute the full N x N pairwise distance matrix once.\n__global__ void computeDistanceMatrixKernel(const Point* __restrict__ points,\n                                             double* __restrict__ dist,\n                                             int N) {\n    int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    int j = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (i < N && j < N) {\n        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;\n        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;\n        dist[static_cast<size_t>(i) * N + j] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n    }\n}\n\n// GPU kernel: one block per candidate seed. Grows a greedy candidate\n// cluster from that seed using the precomputed distance matrix, with the\n// \"find next best point\" step parallelized across the block's threads.\n__global__ void growCandidateClustersKernel(const double* __restrict__ dist,\n                                             const bool* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                             int N,\n                                             double threshold,\n                                             int* __restrict__ members_buf,\n                                             bool* __restrict__ in_cluster_buf,\n                                             int* __restrict__ cardinality_out) {\n    const int seed = blockIdx.x;\n\n    if (clustered[seed]) {\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinality_out[seed] = 0;\n        return;\n    }\n\n    int* members = members_buf + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * N;\n    bool* in_cluster = in_cluster_buf + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * N;\n\n    // Reset this seed's in-cluster mask (parallelized across the block).\n    for (int c = threadIdx.x; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n        in_cluster[c] = false;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n        members[0] = seed;\n        in_cluster[seed] = true;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    extern __shared__ unsigned char smem[];\n    double* best_val = reinterpret_cast<double*>(smem);\n    int* best_idx = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_val + blockDim.x);\n\n    int count = 1;\n    while (count < N) {\n        double local_best_val = DBL_MAX;\n        int local_best_idx = -1;\n\n        for (int c = threadIdx.x; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n            if (clustered[c] || in_cluster[c]) continue;\n\n            const double* row = dist + static_cast<size_t>(c) * N;\n            double max_dist = 0.0;\n            for (int m = 0; m < count; ++m) {\n                const double d = row[members[m]];\n                if (d > max_dist) max_dist = d;\n            }\n\n            if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < local_best_val) {\n                local_best_val = max_dist;\n                local_best_idx = c;\n            }\n        }\n\n        best_val[threadIdx.x] = local_best_val;\n        best_idx[threadIdx.x] = local_best_idx;\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) {\n            if (threadIdx.x < s) {\n                if (best_val[threadIdx.x + s] < best_val[threadIdx.x]) {\n                    best_val[threadIdx.x] = best_val[threadIdx.x + s];\n                    best_idx[threadIdx.x] = best_idx[threadIdx.x + s];\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        const int chosen = best_idx[0];\n        if (chosen < 0) break;\n\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n            members[count] = chosen;\n            in_cluster[chosen] = true;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        count++;\n    }\n\n    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n        cardinality_out[seed] = count;\n    }\n}\n\n// GPU kernel: mark the winning cluster's members as clustered.\n__global__ void markClusteredKernel(bool* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                     const int* __restrict__ members,\n                                     int count) {\n    int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < count) {\n        clustered[members[i]] = true;\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm - orchestrates GPU kernels per round.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    if (N == 0) return clusters;\n\n    Point* d_points = nullptr;\n    double* d_dist = nullptr;\n    bool* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    int* d_members = nullptr;\n    bool* d_in_cluster = nullptr;\n    int* d_cardinality = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dist, static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(bool)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_in_cluster, static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(bool)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinality, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_clustered, 0, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(bool)));\n\n    // Precompute the full pairwise distance matrix once.\n    {\n        dim3 block(16, 16);\n        dim3 grid((N + block.x - 1) / block.x, (N + block.y - 1) / block.y);\n        computeDistanceMatrixKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_points, d_dist, N);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n\n    const int threadsPerBlock = 256;\n    const size_t sharedMemBytes = threadsPerBlock * (sizeof(double) + sizeof(int));\n\n    std::vector<int> min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n                              double* __restrict__ dist,\n                                             int N) {\n    int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    int j = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (i < N && j < N) {\n        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;\n        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;\n        dist[static_cast<size_t>(i) * N + j] = growCandidateClustersKernel(const double* __restrict__ dist,\n                                             const bool* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                             int N,\n                                             double threshold,\n                                             int* __restrict__ members_buf,\n                                             bool* __restrict__ static_cast<size_t>(c) * N;\n            double max_dist = 0.0;\n            for (int m = 0; m < count; ++m) {\n                const double d = row[members[m]];\n                if (d > max_dist)      }\n\n        best_val[threadIdx.x] = local_best_val;\n        best_idx[threadIdx.x] = local_best_idx;\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) {\n            if (threadIdx.x < s) {\n                if (best_val[threadIdx.x + s] < best_val[threadIdx.x]) {\n                    best_val[threadIdx.x] = best_val[threadIdx.x + s];\n                    best_idx[threadIdx.x] = best_idx[threadIdx.x + s];\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        const int chosen = best_idx[0];\n        if (chosen < 0) break;\n\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n            members[count] = chosen;\n            in_cluster[chosen] = true;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        count++;\n    }\n\n    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n        cardinality_out[seed] = count;\n    }\n}\n\n// GPU kernel: mark the winning cluster's members as clustered.\n__global__ void markClusteredKernel(bool* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                     const int* __restrict__ members,\n                                     int count) {\n    int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < count) {\n        clustered[members[i]] = true;\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm - orchestrates GPU kernels per round.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    if (N == 0) return clusters;\n\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dist, static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(bool)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_in_cluster, static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(bool)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinality, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_clustered, 0, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(bool)));\n\n    // Precompute the full pairwise distance matrix once.\n    {\n        dim3 block(16, 16);\n        dim3 grid((N + block.x - 1) / block.x, (N + block.y - 1) / block.y);\n        computeDistanceMatrixKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_points, d_dist, N);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n\n    const int threadsPerBlock = 256;\n    const size_t sharedMemBytes = threadsPerBlock * (sizeof(double) + sizeof(int));\n\n    std::vector<int> (h_cardinality[i] > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = h_cardinality[i];\n                best_seed = i;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (best_seed < 0 || max_cardinality <= 0) {\n            break;\n        }\n\n        std::vector<int> best_members(max_cardinality);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(best_members.data(), d_members + static_cast<size_t>(best_seed) * N,\n best_seed;\n        cluster.members = best_members;\n        clusters.push_back(cluster);\n\n        const int blocks = (max_cardinality + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock;\n 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n    \n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n    \n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : \n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n    \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n    \n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\", \n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n Within a block, the threads cooperate\n//     to scan all remaining candidate points in parallel and reduce to\n//     find the next best point to add (parallel min-reduction).\n//   - After all candidate clusters for the round have been grown, the\n//     host picks the seed producing the largest cluster (matching the\n//     original algorithm's tie-break of first-largest-in-index-order),\n//     commits it, marks its members as clustered on the device, and the\n//     next round begins.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cfloat>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                       \\\n    do {                                                                       \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,   \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__));                                \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                      \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// GPU kernel: compute the full N x N pairwise distance matrix once.\n__global__ void computeDistanceMatrixKernel(const Point* __restrict__ points,\n                                             double* __restrict__ dist,\n                                             int N) {\n    int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    int j = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (i < N && j < N) {\n        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;\n        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;\n        dist[static_cast<size_t>(i) * N + j] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n    }\n}\n\n// GPU kernel: one block per candidate seed. Grows a greedy candidate\n// cluster from that seed using the precomputed distance matrix, with the\n// \"find next best point\" step parallelized across the block's threads.\n__global__ void growCandidateClustersKernel(const double* __restrict__ dist,\n                                             const bool* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                             int N,\n                                             double threshold,\n                                             int* __restrict__ members_buf,\n                                             bool* __restrict__ in_cluster_buf,\n                                             int* __restrict__ cardinality_out) {\n    const int seed = blockIdx.x;\n\n    if (clustered[seed]) {\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinality_out[seed] = 0;\n        return;\n    }\n\n    int* members = members_buf + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * N;\n    bool* in_cluster = in_cluster_buf + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * N;\n\n    // Reset this seed's in-cluster mask (parallelized across the block).\n    for (int c = threadIdx.x; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n        in_cluster[c] = false;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n        members[0] = seed;\n        in_cluster[seed] = true;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    extern __shared__ unsigned char smem[];\n    double* best_val = reinterpret_cast<double*>(smem);\n    int* best_idx = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_val + blockDim.x);\n\n    int count = 1;\n    while (count < N) {\n        double local_best_val = DBL_MAX;\n        int local_best_idx = -1;\n\n        for (int c = threadIdx.x; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n            if (clustered[c] || in_cluster[c]) continue;\n\n            const double* row = dist + static_cast<size_t>(c) * N;\n            double max_dist = 0.0;\n            for (int m = 0; m < count; ++m) {\n                const double d = row[members[m]];\n                if (d > max_dist) max_dist = d;\n            }\n\n            if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < local_best_val) {\n                local_best_val = max_dist;\n                local_best_idx = c;\n            }\n        }\n\n        best_val[threadIdx.x] = local_best_val;\n        best_idx[threadIdx.x] = local_best_idx;\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) {\n            if (threadIdx.x < s) {\n                if (best_val[threadIdx.x + s] < best_val[threadIdx.x]) {\n                    best_val[threadIdx.x] = best_val[threadIdx.x + s];\n                    best_idx[threadIdx.x] = best_idx[threadIdx.x + s];\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        const int chosen = best_idx[0];\n        if (chosen < 0) break;\n\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n            members[count] = chosen;\n            in_cluster[chosen] = true;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        count++;\n    }\n\n    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n        cardinality_out[seed] = count;\n    }\n}\n\n// GPU kernel: mark the winning cluster's members as clustered.\n__global__ void markClusteredKernel(bool* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                     const int* __restrict__ members,\n                                     int count) {\n    int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < count) {\n        clustered[members[i]] = true;\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm - orchestrates GPU kernels per round.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    if (N == 0) return clusters;\n\n    Point* d_points = nullptr;\n    double* d_dist = nullptr;\n    bool* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    int* d_members = nullptr;\n    bool* d_in_cluster = nullptr;\n    int* d_cardinality = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dist, static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(bool)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_in_cluster, static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(bool)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinality, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_clustered, 0, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(bool)));\n\n    // Precompute the full pairwise distance matrix once.\n    {\n        dim3 block(16, 16);\n        dim3 grid((N + block.x - 1) / block.x, (N + block.y - 1) / block.y);\n        computeDistanceMatrixKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_points, d_dist, N);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n\n    const int threadsPerBlock = 256;\n    const size_t sharedMemBytes = threadsPerBlock * (sizeof(double) + sizeof(int));\n\n    std::vector<int> h_cardinality(N);\n    int num_unclustered = N;\n\n    while (num_unclustered > 0) {\n        growCandidateClustersKernel<<<N, threadsPerBlock, sharedMemBytes>>>(\n            d_dist, d_clustered, N, (h_cardinality[i] > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = h_cardinality[i];\n                best_seed = i;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (best_seed < 0 || max_cardinality <= 0) {\n            break;\n        }\n\n        std::vector<int> best_members(max_cardinality);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(best_members.data(), d_members + static_cast<size_t>(best_seed) * N,\n Within a block, the threads cooperate\n//     to scan all remaining candidate points in parallel and reduce to\n//     find the next best point to add (parallel min-reduction).\n//   - After all candidate clusters for the round have been grown, the\n//     host picks the seed producing the largest cluster (matching the\n//     original algorithm's tie-break of first-largest-in-index-order),\n//     commits it, marks its members as clustered on the device, and the\n//     next round begins.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cfloat>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                       \\\n    do {                                                                       \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,   \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__));                                \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                      \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// GPU kernel: compute the full N x N pairwise distance matrix once.\n__global__ void computeDistanceMatrixKernel(const Point* __restrict__ points,\n                                             double* __restrict__ dist,\n                                             int N) {\n    int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    int j = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (i < N && j < N) {\n        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;\n        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;\n        dist[static_cast<size_t>(i) * N + j] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n    }\n}\n\n// GPU kernel: one block per candidate seed. Grows a greedy candidate\n// cluster from that seed using the precomputed distance matrix, with the\n// \"find next best point\" step parallelized across the block's threads.\n__global__ void growCandidateClustersKernel(const double* __restrict__ dist,\n                                             const bool* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                             int N,\n                                             double threshold,\n                                             int* __restrict__ members_buf,\n                                             bool* __restrict__ in_cluster_buf,\n                                             int* __restrict__ cardinality_out) {\n    const int seed = blockIdx.x;\n\n    if (clustered[seed]) {\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinality_out[seed] = 0;\n        return;\n    }\n\n    int* members = members_buf + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * N;\n    bool* in_cluster = in_cluster_buf + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * N;\n\n    // Reset this seed's in-cluster mask (parallelized across the block).\n    for (int c = threadIdx.x; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n        in_cluster[c] = false;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n        members[0] = seed;\n        in_cluster[seed] = true;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    extern __shared__ unsigned char smem[];\n    double* best_val = reinterpret_cast<double*>(smem);\n    int* best_idx = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_val + blockDim.x);\n\n    int count = 1;\n    while (count < N) {\n        double local_best_val = DBL_MAX;\n        int local_best_idx = -1;\n\n        for (int c = threadIdx.x; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n            if (clustered[c] || in_cluster[c]) continue;\n\n            // Distance matrix is symmetric: read dist[member*N + c] instead of\n            // dist[c*N + member] so that consecutive threads (consecutive c)\n            // access consecutive memory addresses (coalesced global loads).\n            double max_dist = 0.0;\n            for (int m = 0; m < count; ++m) {\n                const double d = dist[static_cast<size_t>(members[m]) * N + c];\n                if (d > max_dist) max_dist = d;\n            }\n\n            if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < local_best_val) {\n                local_best_val = max_dist;\n                local_best_idx = c;\n            }\n        }\n\n        best_val[threadIdx.x] = local_best_val;\n        best_idx[threadIdx.x] = local_best_idx;\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) {\n            if (threadIdx.x < s) {\n                if (best_val[threadIdx.x + s] < best_val[threadIdx.x]) {\n                    best_val[threadIdx.x] = best_val[threadIdx.x + s];\n                    best_idx[threadIdx.x] = best_idx[threadIdx.x + s];\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        const int chosen = best_idx[0];\n        if (chosen < 0) break;\n\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n            members[count] = chosen;\n            in_cluster[chosen] = true;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        count++;\n    }\n\n    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n        cardinality_out[seed] = count;\n    }\n}\n\n// GPU kernel: mark the winning cluster's members as clustered.\n__global__ void markClusteredKernel(bool* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                     const int* __restrict__ members,\n                                     int count) {\n    int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i < count) {\n        clustered[members[i]] = true;\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm - orchestrates GPU kernels per round.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    if (N == 0) return clusters;\n\n    Point* d_points = nullptr;\n    double* d_dist = nullptr;\n    bool* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    int* d_members = nullptr;\n    bool* d_in_cluster = nullptr;\n    int* d_cardinality = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dist, static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(bool)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_in_cluster, static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(bool)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinality, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(Point),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_clustered, 0, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(bool)));\n\n    // Precompute the full pairwise distance matrix once.\n    {\n        dim3 block(16, 16);\n        dim3 grid((N + block.x - 1) / block.x, (N + block.y - 1) / block.y);\n        computeDistanceMatrixKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_points, d_dist, N);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n\n    const int threadsPerBlock = 256;\n    const size_t sharedMemBytes = threadsPerBlock * (sizeof(double) + sizeof(int));\n\n    std::vector<int> h_cardinality(N);\n    int num_unclustered = N;\n\n    while (num_unclustered > 0) {\n        growCandidateClustersKernel<<<N, threadsPerBlock, sharedMemBytes>>>(\n            d_dist, d_clustered, N, threshold, d_members, d_in_cluster, d_cardinality);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_cardinality.data(), d_cardinality, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n            if (h_cardinality[i] > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = h_cardinality[i];\n                best_seed = i;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (best_seed < 0 || max_cardinality <= 0) {\n            break;\n        }\n\n        std::vector<int> best_members(max_cardinality);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(best_members.data(), d_members + static_cast<size_t>(best_seed) * N,\n                              static_cast<size_t>(max_cardinality) * sizeof(int),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n        cluster.members = best_members;\n        clusters.push_back(cluster);\n\n        const int blocks = (max_cardinality + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock;\n        markClusteredKernel<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock>>>(\n            d_clustered, d_members + static_cast<size_t>(best_seed) * N, max_cardinality);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        num_unclustered -= max_cardinality;\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_points));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dist));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_clustered));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_members));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_in_cluster));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cardinality));\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\",\n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n\n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n\n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n\n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n\n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n\n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 :\n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n\n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\",\n           total_clustered, num_points,\n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n\n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\",\n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return
